(function initializeOracleV2(){
"use strict";
const data=oracleKnowledge,cfg=oracleUIConfig,root=document.getElementById("site-root"),shell=document.getElementById("or-shell");
if(!root||!shell||shell.dataset.initialized==="true")return;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const siteExportNodes=document.querySelectorAll("#export-site");
if(siteExportNodes.length!==1||siteExportNodes[0].tagName!=="BUTTON"||siteExportNodes[0].type!=="button"||!root.contains(siteExportNodes[0]))throw Error("Original public site export button must be unique within the source root");

const titles={daily:"今日提醒",draw:"問一個問題",reading:"這次的提醒",cards:"欣賞與查牌",knowledge:"神諭卡知識",sources:"文獻與出處",journal:"我的手札",about:"閱讀與練習",reader:"閱讀知識",ai:"公開資料"};
for(const id of ["or-main","or-view-title","or-status","or-sidebar","or-sidebar-close","or-menu-toggle","or-menu-shade",...Object.keys(titles).map(key=>"or-pane-"+key)])if(!$(id))throw Error("Oracle shell node missing: "+id);
const adapterViews=["daily","draw","reading","cards","journal","about"],bindings=[];
for(const view of adapterViews){const selectors=cfg.adapters[view];
if(!Array.isArray(selectors)||!selectors.length)throw Error("Missing Oracle view adapter: "+view);
for(const selector of selectors){const found=[...document.querySelectorAll(selector)];
if(found.length!==1)throw Error("Oracle adapter must match once: "+view+" "+selector);
bindings.push({view,node:found[0],initialHidden:found[0].hidden});
}}
const originalNodes=bindings.map(x=>x.node);
if(!Array.isArray(cfg.pagination)||cfg.pagination.length!==0)throw Error("Preserve the original gallery/history pagers: config.pagination must be []");
const actionBindings=(cfg.actions||[]).map(action=>{if(action.navigationSafe!==true)throw Error("Original action must be navigation safe");
const nodes=[...document.querySelectorAll(action.selector)];
if(nodes.length!==1||!titles[action.view])throw Error("Invalid original action adapter");
return {action,node:nodes[0]};
});
if(new Set(originalNodes).size!==originalNodes.length||originalNodes.some(a=>originalNodes.some(b=>a!==b&&a.contains(b))))throw Error("Oracle adapters overlap; original event flow needs exact mapping.");
const unitMap=new Map(data.units.map(x=>[x.id,x])),sourceMap=new Map(data.sources.map(x=>[x.id,x])),fullSourceIDs=new Set(data.sources.map(x=>x.id)),aliases=new Map(Object.entries(data.aliases||{}));

const clean=v=>String(v==null?"":v),fold=v=>clean(v).normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase(),isHTTP=v=>/^https?:\/\/[^\s]+$/i.test(clean(v));
const element=(tag,className,text)=>{const n=document.createElement(tag);
if(className)n.className=className;
if(text!=null)n.textContent=clean(text);
return n;
};
const button=(text,action,secondary=false)=>{const n=element("button","or-button"+(secondary?" secondary":""),text);
n.type="button";
n.addEventListener("click",action);
return n;
};

function publicRoute(next,params={}){if(offline)return;
const url=new URL(host.location.href);
url.search="";

url.searchParams.set("view",next);
if(params.topic&&unitMap.has(params.topic))url.searchParams.set("topic",params.topic);
if(params.source&&(sourceMap.has(resolveId(params.source))||/^[A-Z]\d{2,6}$/.test(params.source)))url.searchParams.set("source",resolveId(params.source));
host.history.replaceState(null,"",url);
}
function show(next,options={}){if(!titles[next])next="daily";
if(next!=="reader")readerToken++;
view=next;
for(const [key]of Object.entries(titles))$("or-pane-"+key).hidden=key!==next;
for(const b of shell.querySelectorAll("[data-or-view]")){if(b.dataset.orView===next)b.setAttribute("aria-current","page");
else b.removeAttribute("aria-current");
} $("or-view-title").querySelector("span").textContent=titles[next];
setMenu(false);
$("or-main").scrollTop=0;
if(options.route!==false)publicRoute(next,options);
if(options.focus!==false)$("or-pane-"+next).focus({preventScroll:true});
}
function resolveId(id){let steps=0;
while(aliases.has(id)&&steps++<20)id=aliases.get(id);
return id;
}
function status(text){$("or-status").textContent=text;
}
async function getJSON(path,kind){if(offline)throw Error("離線檔保留內附知識與來源；完整文獻目錄請開啟正式網站。");
if(!/^\/[a-z0-9._-]+$/i.test(path))throw Error("Invalid catalog file path");
if(!fetchCache.has(path))fetchCache.set(path,fetch(new URL(path,origin),{cache:"no-store",credentials:"omit"}).then(r=>{if(!r.ok)throw Error("資料載入失敗 "+r.status);
return r.json();
}).then(x=>{if(x.schemaVersion!=="1.0"||x.kind!==kind)throw Error("資料格式不符");
return x;
}).catch(e=>{fetchCache.delete(path);
throw e;
}));
return fetchCache.get(path);
}
async function loadIndex(){if(sourceIndexReady||offline)return;
if(indexPromise)return indexPromise;
indexPromise=(async()=>{for(const path of cfg.externalCatalog.sourceIndexUrls||[]){const json=await getJSON(path,"source_index");
for(const s of json.sources||[]){if(!s.id||!isHTTP(s.url||s.canonicalUrl))continue;
if(!fullSourceIDs.has(s.id))sourceMap.set(s.id,{...sourceMap.get(s.id),...s});
}}if(cfg.externalCatalog.aliasesUrl){const a=await getJSON(cfg.externalCatalog.aliasesUrl,"source_aliases");
for(const [from,to]of Object.entries(a.aliases||{}))aliases.set(from,to);
}sourceIndexReady=true;
})().catch(e=>{indexPromise=null;
throw e;
});
return indexPromise;
}
async function getSource(id){id=resolveId(id);
let s=sourceMap.get(id);
if(s&&fullSourceIDs.has(id))return s;
await loadIndex();
id=resolveId(id);
s=sourceMap.get(id);
if(!s)throw Error("找不到這個來源。");
if(fullSourceIDs.has(id))return s;
if(s.detailShardUrl){const json=await getJSON(s.detailShardUrl,"sources");
for(const full of json.sources||[]){sourceMap.set(full.id,full);
fullSourceIDs.add(full.id);
}
s=sourceMap.get(id)||s;
}return s;
}
function pager(parent,current,total,count,onChange){const n=element("div","or-pager"),pages=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(count/pageSize()));
n.append(element("span","",count+" 筆・第 "+current+" / "+pages+" 頁"));
const controls=element("div");
const prev=button("上一頁",()=>onChange(current-1),true),next=button("下一頁",()=>onChange(current+1),true);
prev.disabled=current<=1;
next.disabled=current>=pages;
controls.append(prev,next);
n.append(controls);
parent.append(n);
}
function makeSelect(heading,values,value,onChange){const n=element("select","or-select");
n.setAttribute("aria-label",heading);
n.append(new Option(heading,""));
for(const v of values)n.append(new Option(label(v),v));
n.value=value;
n.addEventListener("change",()=>onChange(n.value));
return n;
}
function safeLink(text,url){if(!isHTTP(url))return element("span","",text);
const a=element("a","",text);
a.href=url;
a.target="_blank";
a.rel="noopener noreferrer";
return a;
}
function rerender(fn){const active=document.activeElement,key=active&&active.id,start=active&&active.selectionStart,end=active&&active.selectionEnd;
fn();
if(key){const n=$(key);
if(n){n.focus({preventScroll:true});
if(typeof start==="number"&&n.setSelectionRange)n.setSelectionRange(start,end);
}}}
function renderTopics(){const pane=$("or-pane-knowledge");
pane.replaceChildren(element("h2","","讀一點知識，再聽見自己的聯想"));
paragraph(pane,data.units.length+" 篇可查閱文章；每篇保留出處。主題與篇數會持續擴充。");
const tools=element("div","or-toolbar"),input=element("input","or-input");
input.id="or-topic-query";
input.type="search";
input.placeholder="搜尋主題、關鍵字或內文";
input.setAttribute("aria-label","搜尋神諭卡知識");
input.value=topicQuery;
input.addEventListener("input",()=>{topicQuery=input.value;
topicPage=1;
rerender(renderTopics);
});
const filter=makeSelect("所有知識分類",[...new Set(data.units.map(x=>x.category))],topicCategory,v=>{topicCategory=v;
topicPage=1;
rerender(renderTopics);
});
filter.id="or-topic-category";
tools.append(input,filter,button("重設",()=>{topicQuery="";
topicCategory="";
topicPage=1;
renderTopics();
$("or-topic-query").focus();
},true));
pane.append(tools);
const query=fold(topicQuery);
const matches=data.units.filter(x=>(!topicCategory||x.category===topicCategory)&&(!query||fold([x.title,x.summary,...(x.tags||[]),...(x.sections||[]).map(s=>s.heading+" "+s.text)].join(" ")).includes(query)));
topicPage=Math.min(topicPage,Math.max(1,Math.ceil(matches.length/pageSize())));
const grid=element("div","or-grid");
for(const u of matches.slice((topicPage-1)*pageSize(),topicPage*pageSize())){const card=element("article","or-card");
card.append(element("div","or-kicker",u.category),element("h3","",u.title),element("p","or-short",u.summary));
let b;
b=button("閱讀文章",()=>openTopic(u.id,b));
card.append(b);
grid.append(card);
}pane.append(grid);
if(!matches.length)paragraph(pane,"沒有符合的文章，試試其他關鍵字。","or-state");
pager(pane,topicPage,0,matches.length,p=>{topicPage=p;
renderTopics();
show("knowledge");
});
}
function sourceStatus(s){const reviewed=s.status==="checked"&&((s.inspection?.contentOpened===true&&s.inspection?.semanticReviewed===true)||(s.contentOpened===true&&s.semanticReviewed===true));return reviewed?"已核對指定頁面":s.status==="checked"?"來源細節待載入":s.status==="catalogued"?"已整理書目":"待核候選";
}
function sourceClassification(s){const c=s.classification||{};
return [c.domain,...(c.topics||[]),...(c.documentTypes||[])].filter(Boolean).map(label).join("・");
}
function renderSources(){const pane=$("or-pane-sources");
pane.replaceChildren(element("h2","","從哪裡來，讀到哪裡"));
paragraph(pane,"書目、館藏與已核對頁面列在整理目錄；搜尋摘要另列為待核候選。找到出處不代表其中的預測或療癒宣稱已獲證實。");
const modes=element("div","or-tabs");
for(const [key,label]of [["bibliography","整理目錄"],["candidates","待核探索"]]){const b=button(label,()=>{sourceMode=key;
sourcePage=1;
renderSources();
},true);
b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(sourceMode===key));
modes.append(b);
}pane.append(modes);
const values=[...sourceMap.values()],tools=element("div","or-toolbar"),input=element("input","or-input");
input.id="or-source-query";
input.type="search";
input.value=sourceQuery;
input.placeholder="搜尋作者、出版社、館藏或書名";
input.setAttribute("aria-label","搜尋來源書目");
input.addEventListener("input",()=>{sourceQuery=input.value;
sourcePage=1;
rerender(renderSources);
});
const domains=[...new Set(values.map(s=>s.classification&&s.classification.domain).filter(Boolean))].sort();
const languages=[...new Set(values.map(s=>s.publicationLanguage||s.language).filter(Boolean))].sort();
const domain=makeSelect("所有研究分類",domains,sourceDomain,v=>{sourceDomain=v;
sourcePage=1;
rerender(renderSources);
});
domain.id="or-source-domain";
const language=makeSelect("所有出版語言",languages,sourceLanguage,v=>{sourceLanguage=v;
sourcePage=1;
rerender(renderSources);
});
language.id="or-source-language";
tools.append(input,domain,language);
pane.append(tools);
const query=fold(sourceQuery),matches=values.filter(s=>(sourceMode==="candidates"?s.status==="candidate":s.status==="catalogued"||s.status==="checked")&&(!sourceDomain||(s.classification||{}).domain===sourceDomain)&&(!sourceLanguage||(s.publicationLanguage||s.language)===sourceLanguage)&&(!query||fold([s.title,s.author,s.publisher,sourceClassification(s),s.traceability&&s.traceability.libraryName,s.url].join(" ")).includes(query)));
sourcePage=Math.min(sourcePage,Math.max(1,Math.ceil(matches.length/pageSize())));
const grid=element("div","or-grid");
for(const s of matches.slice((sourcePage-1)*pageSize(),sourcePage*pageSize())){const card=element("article","or-card");
card.append(element("div","or-kicker",sourceStatus(s)),element("h3","",s.title),element("p","or-short",[s.author,s.publisher,s.traceability&&s.traceability.libraryName].filter(Boolean).join("・")||sourceClassification(s)||"出版／作者資料待核"));
let b;
b=button("查看出處",()=>openSource(s.id,b));
card.append(b);
grid.append(card);
}pane.append(grid);
if(!matches.length)paragraph(pane,"目前沒有符合的來源。","or-state");
pager(pane,sourcePage,0,matches.length,p=>{sourcePage=p;
renderSources();
show("sources");
});
if(offline)paragraph(pane,"離線檔保留 "+data.sources.length+" 筆內附來源；完整待核目錄請至正式網站。");
}
let readerBack=null;
function readerFrame(title,kicker,back){const pane=$("or-pane-reader");
pane.replaceChildren();
const box=element("article","or-reader"),head=element("div","or-reader-head");
head.append(button("返回",()=>{readerToken++;
back();
},true),element("span","or-kicker",kicker));
box.append(head,element("h2","or-reader-title",title));
pane.append(box);
return box;
}
function listReturn(next,trigger){return()=>{show(next);
if(trigger&&trigger.isConnected)trigger.focus({preventScroll:true});
};
}
function openTopic(id,trigger,tab="summary",section=0,customBack=null){const u=unitMap.get(id);
if(!u)return;
readerToken++;
const back=customBack||listReturn("knowledge",trigger);
readerBack=back;
const box=readerFrame(u.title,u.category,back),tabs=element("div","or-tabs");
for(const [key,label]of [["summary","摘要"],["body","正文"],["sources","來源"]]){const b=button(label,()=>openTopic(id,trigger,key,0,back),true);
b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(tab===key));
tabs.append(b);
}box.append(tabs);
if(tab==="summary"){paragraph(box,u.summary,"or-prose");
paragraph(box,(u.tags||[]).join("・"));
}else if(tab==="body"){const sections=u.sections||[];
section=Math.max(0,Math.min(section,sections.length-1));
if(sections[section]){box.append(element("h3","",sections[section].heading),element("p","or-prose",sections[section].text));
const nav=element("div","or-pager"),prev=button("上一段",()=>openTopic(id,trigger,tab,section-1,back),true),next=button("下一段",()=>openTopic(id,trigger,tab,section+1,back),true);
prev.disabled=section<=0;
next.disabled=section>=sections.length-1;
nav.append(prev,element("span","","第 "+(section+1)+" / "+sections.length+" 段"),next);
box.append(nav);
}}else{const list=element("ul","or-source-list");
for(const sourceId of u.sourceIds||[]){const s=sourceMap.get(resolveId(sourceId)),li=element("li");
let b;
b=button(s?s.title:sourceId,()=>openSource(sourceId,b,()=>openTopic(id,trigger,"sources",0,back)),true);
li.append(b);
list.append(li);
}box.append(list);
}const related=[...new Set(u.relatedTopicIds||[])].map(key=>unitMap.get(key)).filter(other=>other&&other.id!==u.id);
if(related.length){const more=element("details","or-related"),heading=element("summary","","延伸閱讀（"+related.length+"）"),links=element("nav","or-tabs");
links.setAttribute("aria-label","延伸閱讀");
for(const other of related){const link=element("a","or-button",other.title),url=new URL("/oracle-room.html",origin);
url.searchParams.set("view","reader");url.searchParams.set("topic",other.id);link.href=url.href;
link.addEventListener("click",event=>{if(event.button!==0||event.metaKey||event.ctrlKey||event.shiftKey||event.altKey)return;
event.preventDefault();openTopic(other.id,link,"summary",0,()=>openTopic(id,trigger,tab,section,back));});
links.append(link);
}more.append(heading,links);box.append(more);
}show("reader",{topic:id});
}
async function openSource(id,trigger,customBack=null){const token=++readerToken,back=customBack||listReturn("sources",trigger);
readerBack=back;
let box=readerFrame("正在取得來源資料","文獻與出處",back);
paragraph(box,"正在載入…","or-state");
show("reader",{source:id});
try{const s=await getSource(id);
if(token!==readerToken)return;
box=readerFrame(s.title,sourceStatus(s),back);
const dl=element("dl","or-meta"),add=(key,value)=>{if(value===null||value===undefined||value==="")value="未提供／尚未核對";
dl.append(element("dt","",key),element("dd","",Array.isArray(value)?value.join("；"):value));
};
add("來源狀態",sourceStatus(s));
add("作者",s.author);
add("出版社",s.publisher);
add("出版語言",label(s.publicationLanguage||s.language));
add("取用範圍",s.accessScope||s.access);
const dates=s.dates||{};
add("此版本出版",dates.editionPublished);
if(s.legacyBibliographicYear)add("書目年份（類型未明）",s.legacyBibliographicYear.year);
add("網頁發表",dates.webPublished);
add("網頁更新",dates.webUpdated);
const trace=s.traceability||{};
add("來源類型",label(trace.kind||s.type));
add("圖書館",trace.libraryName);
add("館藏紀錄",trace.catalogRecordId);
add("館藏確認",trace.holdingConfirmed===true?"已確認持有":trace.holdingConfirmed===false?"未以此紀錄確認持有":null);
const facts=s.bibliographicFacts||{};
add("ISBN",trace.isbn13||facts.isbn13||facts.isbn10);
add("書頁數",facts.pageCount);
add("牌卡張數",facts.cardCount);
box.append(dl);
box.append(safeLink("前往原始出處",s.url||s.canonicalUrl));
if(s.note)paragraph(box,s.note,"or-prose");
if(s.bibliographicNotes)paragraph(box,s.bibliographicNotes,"or-prose");
if(trace.basis&&trace.basis.length)paragraph(box,"可追溯依據："+trace.basis.join("；"),"or-prose");
if(s.inspection&&s.inspection.semanticReviewed){for(const claim of s.inspection.claimsSupported||[])paragraph(box,claim,"or-prose");
for(const limitation of s.inspection.limitations||[])paragraph(box,"取用限制："+limitation);
}else if(s.status==="candidate"){paragraph(box,"以下為搜尋結果摘要，尚未作為已核對的文章依據。");
if(s.searchSnippet)paragraph(box,s.searchSnippet,"or-prose");
}paragraph(box,"書目與持有紀錄能確認資料的出處；占卜效果與書中主張仍需另行驗證。");
publicRoute("reader",{source:s.id});
$("or-pane-reader").focus({preventScroll:true});
}catch(e){if(token!==readerToken)return;
box.replaceChildren(element("h2","","暫時無法載入來源"),element("p","or-prose",e.message),button("返回",back,true));
}}
function renderAI(){const pane=$("or-pane-ai");
pane.replaceChildren(element("h2","","給閱讀工具與 AI 使用的公開資料"));
paragraph(pane,"知識文章、可追溯來源與搜尋輪次有固定 ID 和 JSON 格式。依取用範圍區分書目、已核對頁面與待核候選。");
const links=element("div","or-ai-links");
for(const [label,path]of [["資料清單","/oracle-data-manifest.json"],["完整知識","/oracle-knowledge-topics.json"],["機器閱讀說明","/llms.txt"],["原創解讀資料","/oracle-reading-manifest.json"],["三牌關係查閱","/oracle-relations.html"]]){const a=safeLink(label,new URL(path,origin).href);
a.className="or-button secondary";
links.append(a);
}siteExportButton.classList.add("or-button","secondary");
links.append(siteExportButton);
pane.append(links);
paragraph(pane,"完整來源索引採分檔；請由資料清單讀取所有 source_index 檔，並依 detailShardUrl 開啟來源的完整核對範圍。","or-prose");
const indexLinks=element(
